Coordinate descent implementation details:
`_coord_minimize(a, r, M_current maybe)`:
- n=len(a); u=a[r]. If 1-u <= 1e-12: skip.
- Compute cross contributions h_old (length L=2n-1): zeros; idx = slice(r, r+n); if r < L: h_old[r:r+n] = a[r:r+n]? Wait a index k-r. If r=0, k=0..n-1 → a[0..n-1]. If r>0, k=r..r+n-1 → a[0..n-1]. Good. Then set h_old[2*r] = 0 if 2*r<L (self term). alpha array? For objective d, alpha_k is 1 only if 2r=k; else 0.
- c known. gamma = (c - 2u(1-u)*h_old - u^2*alpha_arr) / (1-u)^2. Need alpha_arr length L with 1 at k=2r. Could avoid array by computing numerator = c.copy(); numerator[r:r+n] -= 2u(1-u)*a[r:r+n]; if 2r<L numerator[2r] +=? Actually for k=2r: current c includes u^2 + cross terms with others? The formula subtracts 2u(1-u)*h_old (h_old self zero). The u^2 alpha should be subtracted separately. So numerator = c - 2u(1u)*h_old; if 2r<L: numerator[2r] -= u*u. But current c at k=2r also includes cross terms 2u a_j where 2r-j? Wait if k=2r, pairs (j, k-j) with j≠r: a_j a_{2r-j}. The formula after removing r: γ constant includes pairs among others with sum 2r. Cross_old at k=2r would include a_{r} for j=r? It should be zero; pairs with one r are self u^2 only, other one r? Pair (r, 2r-r=r) only self. So subtract u^2. Good.
- h_new = h_old/(1-u), self zero.
But note for k where k-r valid, h_old=a[k-r]. For k=2r, h_old self set zero, but there are also pairs (r, j) with j? Wait if coordinate r and another j, sum =2r => j=r. So no. Good.
- Define objective for m: max over k of (1-m)^2 gamma + 2m(1-m) h_new + alpha_arr m^2.
We can evaluate M(m) quickly using numpy arrays: f = gamma*(1-m)^2 + 2*h_new*m*(1-m) + alpha_arr*m^2; return max(f). But for coordinate minimization, we can avoid alpha_arr array by start f=gamma*(...)+2*h_new*m*(1-m); if 2r<L set f[2r]+=m*m.

Let's design each helper carefully.

Flatten normalize:
```
def _norm(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, float)
    a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <1e-300: return np.ones(len(a))/len(a)
    return a/s
```
But objective scale invariant, sum=1 for optimization. Return can be same.

Score exact:
```
def _score_exact(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); n=len(a); s=a.sum();
    if s<0.01: return inf
    c=_fft_convolve(a); M=np.max(c)
    return 2*n*M/s/s
```
Use _fft_convolve normalized scale.

FFT convolve:
```
def _fft_convolve(a):
    n=len(a); size=2*n-1; N=1<<(size-1).bit_length();
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N); return c[:size]
```
For large n, okay.

Interpolate:
```
def _interp(a, n):
    if len==n return norm
    x=np.linspace(0,1,len(a)); out=np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n),x,a); out=max; norm
```
Need preserve score; interpolation of normalized sum1.

def _corr_pa(a,p): # p length 2n-1, returns r_i=Σ_j a_j p_{i+j}
    n=len(a); L=len(p)
    pr = p[::-1]
    N=1 << (n+L-2).bit_length()? Wait conv length n+L-1. size=n+L-1. N=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); P=np.fft.rfft(pr,N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(A*P,N)
    idx = L-1 - np.arange(n)
    return conv[idx]
```
Check size: conv length = n + L -1 = n+2n-1-1? L=2n-1 =>3n-2. Max last index 3n-3. Need N >=3n-2. `size = n + L - 1`; `N=1 << (size-1).bit_length()`. idx max L-1 =2n-2, min 0? Optimization run:
```
def _lbfgs_opt(a0, beta_list, deadline):
    global _LBFGS_A,_LBFGS_BETA
    a0 = _norm(a0)
    theta0 = np.log(a0 + 1e-15) # could be large negative; subtract mean to avoid
    theta0 -= theta0.mean()
    best = a0.copy(); best_score=_score_exact(a0)
    for beta in beta_list:
        if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
        _LBFGS_A = a0
        _LBFGS_BETA = beta
        # maxiter based on n and beta? e.g. 200
        maxiter = 300
        try:
            res = minimize(_lbfgs_f, theta0, jac=_lbfgs_g, method='L-BFGS-B',
                           options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxfun':10000, 'disp':False})
        except: pass
        theta = res.x if success? even if not.
        a = softmax(theta)
        a = _norm(a)
        s = _score_exact(a)
        if s<best_score: best...
        a0 = a? Use res run for next beta.
    return best, best_score
```

Implementation of golden:
```
def _golden_min(f, lo, hi, iters=30):
    gr = (np.sqrt(5)-1)/2
    c = hi - gr*(hi-lo); d = lo + gr*(hi-lo)
    fc=f(c); fd=f(d)
    for _ in range(iters):
       if fc < fd: hi=d; d=c; fd=fc; c=hi-gr*(hi-lo); fc=f(c)
       else: lo=c; c=d; fc=fd; d=lo+gr*(hi-lo); fd=f(d)
    return 0.5*(lo+hi)
```
Need f scalar fast. We can create `_coord_eval_m(gamma,h,self_idx,m)` top-level. But passing arrays each scalar overhead. Within coordinate, we can define local function? No closures/nesting. But to avoid function call overhead, write helper top-level.
```
def _coord_eval(gamma, h, idx, m):
    one=1-m
    val = gamma*one*one + 2*h*m*one
    val[idx] += m*m
    return float(np.max(val))
```

```
def _coord_eval_vec(gamma,h,idx,ms):
    ms=np.asarray(ms)[:,None]
    one=1-ms
    val=gamma*one*one + 2*h*ms*one
    # add m^2 to idx for each: val[idx, :] += ms[0,:]^2? idx scalar row
    val[idx, :] += ms[0, :]**2
    return np.max(val, axis=1)
```

Now, active-subgradient stage maybe can be implemented using same gradient at high β and line search in parameter blending with a scalar random direction? Let's design simple:
```
def _smooth_line_search(a, beta, deadline, iterations):
   a = _norm(a)
   best = a copy
   for it:
      if time>deadline break
      theta=log(a+eps)
      F,grad=_ls_value_grad(theta,beta)
      # normalize grad to norm 1
      gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
      if gnorm<1e-12: break
      d = -grad/gnorm
      # find best alpha over logspace(-4,2,17) maybe plus small
      base_theta = np.log(a+1e-300)
      best_alpha=0; best_score=_score_exact(a)
      for alpha in alphas:
         theta_try=base_theta+alpha*d
         a_try=softmax(theta_try)
         s=_score_exact(a_try)
         if s<best_score: update
      if no improvement: break/halve alpha range
      a=best_a
   return best
```
This uses exact score line search. Could escape LBFGS local if direction not too small. But LBFGS already does line search. Maybe not needed.

Maybe we can exploit **Fourier domain**: Objective max of c_k can be approximated by L∞ of coefficients = distance from ideal. The maximum coefficient can be bounded by integral of |P(e^{iθ})|^2? We know c are coefficients of P^2. The l∞ of coefficients can be estimated by antialiasing: c_k = (1/2π)∫ P(e^{iθ})^2 e^{-ikθ} dθ. If P^2 has bandlimited spectrum? Not.

Could optimize `max_k c_k` via spectral factorization? If we choose P(z) such that P(z)^2 = T(z) - R(z) with T flat and R low? Hmm.

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size)/max(1,a.size)
    return a/s
```
For final, if length one? okay.

`_fft_convolve`: use rfft. If n=1 size1, bit_length? `(size-1).bit_length()` for size=1: 0 -> 1<<0=1. good.

`_ls_value`:
```
def _ls_value(theta, beta):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(theta)
    t = theta - np.max(theta)
    t = np.maximum(t, -700.0)
    e = np.exp(t)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(theta)
    a = e/s
    c = _fft_convolve(a)
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    shifted = beta*(c - maxc)
    # beta maybe inf? no
    p = np.exp(shifted)
    Z = p.sum()
    if Z <=0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
       # if overflow? shifted <=0 so no
       return 1e100, np.zeros_like(theta)
    F = maxc + math.log(Z)
    r = _corr_pa(a, p)
    # r should length n
    S = float(np.dot(a, r))
    grad = (2.0 * beta / Z) * a * (r - S)
    # clean nonfinite
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros_like(theta)
    # clip grad norm
    gn = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
    if gn > 1e6:
        grad = grad / gn * 1e6
    return F, grad
```
Could there be large `beta/Z` if Z ~n? β up to 1e5, Z ~n? p sum about number active times? If one peak, Z~1; if smooth, Z~n. okay.

`_lbfgs_fun`:
```
def _lbfgs_fun(x):
   val, _ = _ls_value(x, _LBFGS_BETA)
   return val
```
`_lbfgs_g` similar.
`_lbfgs_callback`: returns True if time deadline. `return time.monotonic() > _LBFGS_DEADLINE`

In scipy minimize, if callback returns True, it stops? Need check. For L-BFGS-B, callback can return bool in recent SciPy. To be safe, if not supported, it may ignore. We'll set maxiter limited so not overshoot dramatically. But callback no harm.

`_lbfgs_opt(a0, beta_list, deadline)`:
```
def _lbfgs_opt(a0, beta_list, deadline):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_s=_score(a)
    print(f"      LBFGS start s={best_s:.8f}")
    # initial theta: log floor
    eps=1e-12
    theta=np.log(a + eps)
    theta -= np.mean(theta)
    for beta in beta_list:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       if beta is None: continue
       # Estimate maxiter: maybe max(20, int((deadline-now)*20)) but cap 300? We can dynamic.
       global _LBFGS_BETA, _LBFGS_DEADLINE
       _LBFGS_BETA = float(beta)
       _LBFGS_DEADLINE = deadline
       # use options; maxiter 400, maxfun 5000
       try:
          res = minimize(_lbfgs_fun, theta, jac=_lbfgs_g, method='L-BFGS-B',
                         options={'maxiter':400, 'maxfun':800, 'ftol':1e-14, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':50, 'disp':False})
       except Exception as ex:
          print(f"      LBFGS beta {beta} failed: {ex}")
       # if result x finite
       if hasattr(res,'x') and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
          theta_new = res.x
          a_new = _softmax(theta_new)
          a_new = _normalize(a_new)
          s_new = _score(a_new)
          if np.isfinite(s_new) and s_new < best_s - 1e-12:
             best_s=s_new; best_a=a_new.copy()
          theta = theta_new
          # Also maybe if no improvement but beta done, continue
          print(f"      LBFGS beta={beta:.0f} score={best_s:.8f}")
       else:
          print("      LBFGS bad result")
    return best_a, best_s
```
maxfun 800 maybe too low for high dimension; but time. L-BFGS-B may evaluate gradient many times; 800 evaluate maybe few seconds. We'll run multiple beta. Could set maxiter=200, maxfun=400. Use subdeadline; but if callback not stop, maxfun controls. To use more time, 800*4 betas=3200 evals. Each eval FFT+corr ~0.5ms ->1.6s. Good.
But for n=1000, maybe 10ms each ->32s. okay.
We can choose `maxfun_per_beta=300` for larger n? Could pass. Let's set options dynamic based on n: if n<=600 maxfun 600, elif 800 maxfun 300, else 200. Simpler use global.

def _topk_direction(a, c, K):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    order=np.argpartition(-c, min(K,L)-1)[:K] maybe. If K>=L all.
    # weights: uniform over top K or weighted by c? Use p = (c[order] - min?) maybe.
    vals = c[order]
    # weights normalized: maybe vals**4 to emphasize peaks, or uniform. Try uniform.
    w = np.ones(K)/K
    p=np.zeros(L); p[order]=w
    r=_corr_pa(a,p)
    S=dot(a,r)
    d = a*(S-r)  # descent direction
    d = d - a*np.dot(a,d)? For softmax gradient, direction should preserve sum scale? d = a*(S-r) automatically sum=0? Σ a_i d_i =Σ a_i^2(S-r)=S*? not necessarily zero. For θ gradient, adding constant to θ no effect, so we can center d: d = d - np.mean(d)? Since θ direction constant irrelevant. But d from gradient has sum? Since Σa=1. So sum d=0. Good.
    return p,r,d

def _sgd_line(a, beta? or K, deadline, max_iters=20):
    a=_normalize(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_s=_score(a); c=_fft_convolve(a)
    alphas = np.concatenate([np.arange(0,10,0.5?), np.logspace(-4,2,25)][...])
    scale? d norm unit. Use d normalized to norm 1.
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time>deadline break
       c=_fft_convolve(a); order topK
       choose K based on it maybe 2,4,8,16.
       p,r,d; dnorm; if <1e-12 break; d/=dnorm
       base_theta=np.log(a+1e-300)
       improved=False
       for alpha in alphas:
          theta=base_theta+alpha*d
          a_try=_softmax(theta); a_try=_normalize(a_try)
          s=_score(a_try)
          if s<best_s-1e-12: best... improved=True
       if not improved: break
       a=best
       # maybe reduce alphas range? not needed
    return best,best_s
```
This calls `_score` many times; for K up to 5, alphas 30, max_iters 10 =>1500 FFTs. For n=500 okay maybe 1s. For n=1000 10s. Use sparingly.
Weights p: We can use top K uniform; for K=1 exact max sensitivity. For K multiple, average. Could also use p = c^pow. Let's implement `_topk_direction` with K and weight_power param. If weight_power=0 uniform; >0 `w = vals**(power+1)?` to avoid zeros. Use pow=2 maybe. We can call with K in [1,2,4,8,16,32]. This is like active-subgradient.
But L-BFGS logsumexp already does similar with weights exp. Maybe subgradient redundant. But it uses exact line search and top K, might improve. Keep brief.

Coordinate polish detailed code:
```
def _coord_polish(a, deadline, max_sweeps=200, verbose=True):
    a = _normalize(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_s=_score(a)
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    c=_fft_convolve(a); M=float(np.max(c))
    ms_grid = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,17)
    tol=1e-13
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
       improved=0
       # coordinate order: maybe random? Use np.random default? For deterministic but escape local, use reversal sweeps.
       # Could choose order np.arange(n) then reversed alternating; or random with seed sweep.
       order = np.arange(n)
       for r in order:
          if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2: break
          u = float(a[r])
          s1 = 1.0 - u
          if s1 <= 1e-14 or s1 > 1.0 - 1e-14:
             # if u exactly 1? skip; if s1 close 1 (u tiny) still ok
             if u <= 1e-14: continue
          cf = 2.0*u*s1
          num = c.copy()
          # subtract cf*a over segment r:r+n
          num[r:r+n] -= cf * a
          # fix self:
          idx2 = 2*r
          if idx2 < L:
             num[idx2] += cf*u - u*u
          gamma = num / (s1*s1)
          # remove tiny negatives
          # don't hard clip positive? maybe use np.maximum(-1e-15? We can clip to 0 to avoid negative lower.
          gamma = np.maximum(gamma, 0.0)
          h = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
          h[r:r+n] = a / s1
          if idx2 < L:
             h[idx2] = 0.0
          # candidate m list: grid plus u
          ms = np.concatenate((ms_grid, np.array([u])))
          # Ensure in [0,1]
          # eval vector
          one = 1.0 - ms
          val = gamma[None,:] * (one[:,None]*one[:,None]) + 2.0*h[None,:] * (ms[:,None]*one[:,None])
          if idx2 < L:
             val[idx2,:] += ms*ms
          vals = np.max(val, axis=1)
          # choose best among values
          imin = int(np.argmin(vals))
          m0 = float(ms[imin]); bestcand = float(vals[imin])
          # local refine if bestcand < M - tol
          m_best=m0; M_best=bestcand
          if bestcand < M - tol:
             # bracket
             if m0 < 1e-8:
                lo,hi=0.0,0.25
             elif m0 > 1.0-1e-8:
                lo,hi=0.75,1.0
             else:
                wdt=0.125
                lo=max(0.0,m0-wdt); hi=min(1.0,m0+wdt)
             if hi - lo > 1e-10:
                m_ref = _golden_coord(gamma,h,idx2,lo,hi,25)
                M_ref = _coord_eval_scalar(gamma,h,idx2,m_ref)
                if M_ref < M_best:
                   m_best=m_ref; M_best=M_ref
             # If m_best not much better than M, still check
          if M_best < M - 1e-12:
             one = 1.0 - m_best
             inv_s1 = a / s1
             a_new = one * inv_s1
             a_new[r] = m_best
             # Ensure nonnegative
             a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
             sn = a_new.sum()
             if sn <= 1e-300:
                continue
             a_new = a_new / sn
             # exact c check
             c_new = _fft_convolve(a_new)
             M_new = float(np.max(c_new))
             if M_new < M - 1e-13:
                a = a_new
                c = c_new
                M = M_new
                improved += 1
                s_est = 2.0*n*M
                if s_est < best_s - 1e-12:
                   best_s=s_est; best_a=a.copy()
                if verbose and improved % 25 ==0:
                   print(f"        coord sweep {sweep} improvements={improved} score={best_s:.8f}")
          # else leave c maybe stale? If a unchanged, c stays.
       if improved == 0:
          # no coordinate improvement; maybe stop after trying reverse?
          if sweep > 0:
             break
       # After sweep, recompute c and a normalization to reduce drift
       a = _normalize(a)
       c = _fft_convolve(a)
       M = float(np.max(c))
       s_est=2.0*n*M
       if s_est < best_s - 1e-12:
          best_s=s_est; best_a=a.copy()
    return best_a, best_s
```
Need `_golden_coord` top-level:
```
def _golden_coord(gamma,h,idx,lo,hi,iters):
    gr = (math.sqrt(5)-1)/2
    c = hi - gr*(hi-lo); d = lo + gr*(hi-lo)
    fc = _coord_eval_scalar(gamma,h,idx,c); fd = _coord_eval_scalar(gamma,h,idx,d)
    for _ in range(iters):
       if fc < fd:
          hi=d; d=c; fd=fc
          c=hi - gr*(hi-lo); fc=_coord_eval_scalar(gamma,h,idx,c)
       else:
          lo=c; c=d; fc=fd
          d=lo + gr*(hi-lo); fd=_coord_eval_scalar(gamma,h,idx,d)
    return 0.5*(lo+hi)
```
`_coord_eval_scalar`:
```
def _coord_eval_scalar(gamma,h,idx,m):
    one=1.0-m
    val = gamma*one*one + 2.0*h*m*one
    if 0 <= idx < val.size: val[idx] += m*m
    return float(np.max(val))
```

Implement `_sgd_topk(a, Ks, deadline, max_rounds=20)`:
```
def _sgd_topk(a, Ks, deadline, max_rounds=20):
    a=_normalize(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_s=_score(a)
    alphas = np.unique(np.concatenate((np.logspace(-5,1.0,25), [0.02? no logspace includes] )))
    for round in range(max_rounds):
       if time>deadline: break
       K = Ks[round % len(Ks)]
       c=_fft_convolve(a); p, r, d = _topk_dir(a,c,K)
       d -= np.mean(d) # sum zero
       dn=np.linalg.norm(d)
       if dn<1e-12: break
       d /= dn
       theta=np.log(a+1e-300)
       best_local_s=best_s; best_local_a=best
       improved=False
       for alpha in alphas:
          a_try=_softmax(theta+alpha*d)
          s_try=_score(a_try)
          if s_try<best_s-1e-12:
             best_s=s_try; best=a_try; improved=True
       if not improved:
          break
    return best,best_s
```
Need `_topk_dir` top-level:
```
def _topk_dir(a,c,K):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1
   K=min(K,L)
   order=np.argpartition(-c, K-1)[:K] if K<L else np.arange(L)
   vals=c[order]
   # weights: smooth emphasize peaks: w = vals/(vals+1e-15); or uniform; choose weights = (vals/np.max(vals))**4 to focus highest, then normalize.
   w = (vals / (np.max(vals)+1e-300)) ** 4.0
   w /= w.sum()
   p=np.zeros(L); p[order]=w
   r=_corr_pa(a,p)
   S=dot(a,r)
   d=a*(S-r)
   return p,r,d
```
Using weights may differ from logsumexp. K=1 uniform.

But `_sgd_topk` with exact score line search could be expensive if changes small. Limit max_rounds 30, Ks [1,1,2,2,4,4,8,16,32]. It will stop if no alpha improves. We can call after LBFGS with deadline 120s.

def propose_candidate():
    start = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = start + 2350.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(777)
    best_a = None; best_score = float('inf')
    # load parent
    parent = None
    try:
       parent_seq = height_sequence_1
       arr = np.asarray(parent_seq, dtype=np.float64)
       if arr.ndim ==1 and arr.size>0 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
           parent = _normalize(np.clip(arr,0,1000))
           parent_score = _score(parent)
           if np.isfinite(parent_score): best_a=parent; best_score=parent_score
    except Exception: pass
    if best_a is None:
       parent = _normalize(np.ones(500)); best_score=_score(parent)
    print parent score
    # choose main length
    n_parent = len(parent)
    if 150 <= n_parent <= 700:
       n_main = n_parent
    else:
       n_main = 500
    a_main = _interpolate(best_a, n_main)
    s_main = _score(a_main)
    if s_main < best_score: update
    # Main optimize
    main_deadline = min(hard_deadline-300, time.monotonic()+1800)
    a_opt, s_opt = _optimize_length(a_main, n_main, main_deadline, verbose=True)
    update
    # Try other lengths
    other_lengths = []
    for nn in [400, 500, 600, 800, 1000, 1200]:

    # fallback

    except: verify=float('inf')

Detailed code snippets:

`_fft_convolve`:
```
def _fft_convolve(a):
    n = len(a)
    size = 2*n - 1
    if size <= 1:
        return a.astype(np.float64).copy()
    N = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c[:size])
    # eliminate tiny negative? maybe leave; max could be affected by tiny negative no.
    return c
```

`_corr_pa`:
```
def _corr_pa(a, p):
    n = len(a)
    L = len(p)
    size = n + L - 1
    N = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * P, N)
    idx = L - 1 - np.arange(n)
    r = conv[idx]
    # due roundoff, tiny imaginary removed; maybe clip small?
    return r
```
`p[::-1]` creates view; rfft handles. Good.

`_ls_value` full:
```
def _ls_value(theta, beta):
    try:
       t = theta - np.max(theta)
       t = np.maximum(t, -700.0)
       e = np.exp(t)
       s = e.sum()
       if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): return 1e100, np.zeros_like(theta)
       a = e / s
       c = _fft_convolve(a)
       maxc = float(np.max(c))
       shifted = beta * (c - maxc)
       p = np.exp(shifted)
       Z = float(p.sum())
       if Z <= 0 or not np.isfinite(Z): return 1e100, zeros
       F = maxc + math.log(Z)
       r = _corr_pa(a, p)
       S = float(np.dot(a, r))
       grad = (2.0 * beta / Z) * a * (r - S)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): grad = zeros
       gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
       if gnorm > 1e5: grad *= (1e5/gnorm)
       if not np.isfinite(F): F=1e100
       return F, grad
    except Exception:
       return 1e100, np.zeros_like(theta)
```
If beta negative? no.

`_lbfgs_g` uses `_ls_value`. `_lbfgs_f` too. Potential double checking.

`softmax`:
```
def _softmax(theta):
    t = theta - np.max(theta)
    t = np.maximum(t, -700.0)
    e = np.exp(t)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(len(theta))/len(theta)
    return e/s
```

`_lbfgs_opt`: Need reset global beta before minimize. The objective `_lbfgs_f` reads global. If an exception inside minimize due time? okay.
```
def _lbfgs_opt(a0,betas,deadline,maxfun=500):
    global _LBFGS_BETA,_LBFGS_DEADLINE
    a = _normalize(a0)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_s=_score(a)
    theta = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-15))
    theta -= np.mean(theta)
    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
        b = float(beta)
        if not np.isfinite(b) or b <=0: continue
        _LBFGS_BETA = b
        _LBFGS_DEADLINE = deadline
        options = {'maxiter': 300, 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol': 1e-14, 'gtol': 1e-8, 'maxls': 40, 'disp': False}
        try:
           res = minimize(_lbfgs_f, theta, jac=_lbfgs_g, method='L-BFGS-B', options=options, callback=_lbfgs_callback)
        except Exception as ex:
           print(...)
        if hasattr(res,'x'):
            theta_new = np.asarray(res.x,float)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(theta_new)):
                theta = theta_new
                a_new = _normalize(_softmax(theta_new))
                s_new = _score(a_new)
                if np.isfinite(s_new) and s_new < best_s - 1e-12:
                    best_s=s_new; best_a=a_new.copy()
        print(f"       LBFGS beta={b:.0f} best={best_s:.8f}")
    return best_a,best_s
```
If res.x after callback timeout may be current. Good.
`maxfun` may be int. For n large, set in caller.

`_sgd_topk`:
```
def _topk_dir(a, c, K): top-level
```
Need `np.argpartition` for K=L? If K>=L use all. If K<1? set 1.
Weights exponent: For K=1, w=[1].
```
def _topk_dir(a, c, K):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    K=int(min(max(K,1), L))
    if K >= L:
       order = np.arange(L)
    else:
       order = np.argpartition(-c, K-1)[:K]
    vals = c[order]
    vmax = float(np.max(vals))
    if vmax <= 0: w = np.ones(K)/K
    else:
       w = (vals / vmax) ** 4.0
       sw = w.sum()
       if sw <=0 or not np.isfinite(sw): w=np.ones(K)
       else: w=w/sw
    p = np.zeros(L)
    p[order] = w
    r = _corr_pa(a,p)
    S = float(np.dot(a,r))
    d = a*(S-r)
    return p,r,d
```
Line search:
```
def _sgd_topk(a, k_list, deadline, max_rounds=20):
    a=_normalize(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_s=_score(a)
    alphas = np.unique(np.concatenate((np.logspace(-4, 0.8, 24), np.array([0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0]))))
    alphas = np.sort(alphas)
    for rnd in range(max_rounds):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       K = k_list[rnd % len(k_list)]
       c = _fft_convolve(a)
       p,r,d = _topk_dir(a,c,K)
       d = d - np.mean(d)
       dn = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
       if dn < 1e-14: break
       d /= dn
       theta = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
       improved = False
       for alpha in alphas:
           if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
           a_try = _softmax(theta + alpha*d)
           s_try = _score(a_try)
           if np.isfinite(s_try) and s_try < best_s - 1e-12:
              best_s=s_try; best_a=a_try.copy(); improved=True
       if not improved:
          break
       a = best_a
       c = None # next recompute
    return best_a,best_s
```
This tries all alphas each round, not best step then recompute. Good but may find better than current. If best improves, `a=best_a` for next round.
Could line search over alpha using previous best within round; okay.

`_coord_polish` full. Need use `grid_points` and local candidates. Let's write carefully.
```
def _coord_polish(a, deadline, max_sweeps=100, verbose=False):
    a = _normalize(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_s = _score(a)
    n = len(a)
    L = 2*n - 1
    grid = _coord_grid_points(n)
    G = len(grid)
    tol = 1e-12
    refine_iters = 20 if n <= 1000 else 12
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 3: break
       c = _fft_convolve(a)
       M = float(np.max(c))
       improved = 0
       for r in range(n):
          if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2: break
          u = float(a[r])
          s1 = 1.0 - u
          if u <= 1e-15 or s1 <= 1e-15:
             continue
          num = c.copy()
          num[r:r+n] -= (2.0 * u) * a
          idx2 = 2*r
          num[idx2] += u*u
          gamma = num / (s1*s1)
          # Clip tiny negatives and ensure finite
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(gamma)):
             continue
          gamma = np.where(gamma < 0.0, 0.0, gamma)
          h = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
          h[r:r+n] = a / s1
          h[idx2] = 0.0
          # evaluate grid
          vals = _coord_eval_vec(gamma,h,idx2,grid)
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(vals)):
             continue
          Mg = float(np.min(vals))
          Mgrid = Mg
          Mu = _coord_eval_scalar(gamma,h,idx2,u)
          M_best = min(Mg, Mu, M)
          # If best candidate significantly better than current
          if M_best < M - tol:
             candidates=[]
             # local minima on grid
             for i in range(G):
                if vals[i] < M - tol:
                   left = vals[i-1] if i>0 else np.inf
                   right = vals[i+1] if i<G-1 else np.inf
                   if vals[i] <= left + 1e-15 and vals[i] <= right + 1e-15:
                      if i == 0:
                         lo = 0.0; hi = grid[1] if G>1 else 0.25
                      elif i == G-1:
                         lo = grid[G-2] if G>1 else 0.75; hi = 1.0
                      else:
                         lo = grid[i-1]; hi = grid[i+1]
                      if hi > lo + 1e-9:
                         candidates.append((float(vals[i]), lo, hi))
             # include u
             if Mu < M - tol:
                step = 1.0/(G-1) if G>1 else 0.25
                lo=max(0.0,u-step); hi=min(1.0,u+step)
                if hi > lo + 1e-9:
                   candidates.append((Mu, lo, hi))
             # include global best grid even if not local?
             im = int(np.argmin(vals))
             if vals[im] < M - tol:
                step=1/(G-1)
                lo=max(0.0, grid[im]-step); hi=min(1.0, grid[im]+step)
                candidates.append((float(vals[im]), lo, hi))
             # sort by base val
             if candidates:
                candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])  # wait no lambda!
```
Rules: no lambda functions. Need sort candidates without lambda. Use `candidates.sort(key=lambda ...)` prohibited. Now, `_optimize_length` code with no lambdas:
```
def _optimize_length(a_start, n, deadline, verbose=False):
    a = _interpolate(a_start,n)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_s=_score(a)
    max_cycles = 3 if n <= 700 else 2
    klist = [1,1,2,2,4,4,8,16,32]
    for cyc in range(max_cycles):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30: break
       rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
       M = best_s / (2.0 * n) if n > 0 else 1.0
       if M <= 1e-15: M = 1e-6
       betas = [max(1.0, 5.0/M), max(1.0, 15.0/M), max(1.0, 35.0/M)]
       maxfun = 600 if n<=600 else 350 if n<=1000 else 220
       improved_cycle=False
       alloc_lb = min(rem*0.40, 280.0)
       if alloc_lb > 25:
          a1,s1 = _lbfgs_opt(a,betas,time.monotonic()+alloc_lb,maxfun)
          if np.isfinite(s1):
             a = a1
             if s1 < best_s - 1e-12:
                best_s=s1; best_a=a.copy(); improved_cycle=True
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30: break
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       alloc_sg = min(rem*0.15, 80.0)
       if alloc_sg > 20:
          a2,s2 = _sgd_topk(a,klist,time.monotonic()+alloc_sg,max_rounds=30 if n<=800 else 12)
          if np.isfinite(s2):
             a=a2
             if s2 < best_s - 1e-12:
                best_s=s2; best_a=a.copy(); improved_cycle=True
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30: break
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       alloc_cd = max(0.0, rem - 10.0)
       if alloc_cd > 25:
          msweeps = 300 if n<=700 else 120
          a3,s3 = _coord_polish(a, time.monotonic()+alloc_cd, max_sweeps=msweeps, verbose=verbose)
          if np.isfinite(s3):
             a=a3
             if s3 < best_s - 1e-12:
                best_s=s3; best_a=a.copy(); improved_cycle=True
       else:
          # ensure best
          s_now = _score(a)
          if np.isfinite(s_now) and s_now < best_s: best...
       if not improved_cycle:
          # maybe break; but next cycle might with different beta? We generated based on best. If no improvement, likely local.
          break
       if verbose:
          print(f"     cycle {cyc+1} n={n} score={best_s:.8f}")
    # final exact
    final_s = _score(best_a)
    return best_a, final_s
```
This runs coord after each cycle; if coord converges, next cycle LBFGS may still improve. Good.

   if len(a)==n:
      s=a.sum(); if s<... a=np.ones(n); return a/s

   x=np.linspace(0,1,len(a)); out=np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n), x, a)
   out=np.maximum(out,0)
   return _normalize(out)

Let's review potential mathematical mistakes in gradient:
For softmax, derivative of normalized c: We set a=softmax sum1. F(a)=logsumexp(β c(a)). The gradient w.r.t. θ: `a∘∇_a F - a (Σ a∘∇_a F)` (normal). ∇_a F_i = β/Z Σ_k p_k ∂c_k/∂a_i. For ∂c_k/∂a_i: We derived 2 a_{k-i} if k-i in [0,n-1]. But c_k = Σ_j a_j a_{k-j}. Let's differentiate explicitly with i fixed:
Terms where first index j=i: a_i a_{k-i} if k-i valid. Derivative wrt a_i: a_{k-i}+a_i*0? Actually derivative of a_i b wrt a_i if b depends on a_i? b=a_{k-i}; if k-i != i, b independent -> a_{k-i}. If k-i=i, term a_i^2 derivative 2a_i, formula a_{k-i}=a_i. Second: where second index k-j=i => j=k-i; term a_j a_i. Derivative: a_j if k-i != i, or if same same term included? Wait the sum over j includes both j=i and j=k-i as separate if different, but the expression Σ_j a_j a_{k-j} already includes both ordered pairs. If k-i ≠ i, there are two terms: j=i gives a_i a_{k-i}; j=k-i gives a_{k-i} a_i. Their sum is 2 a_i a_{k-i}. Differentiate: derivative of first wrt a_i = a_{k-i}; derivative of second wrt a_i = a_{k-i}; total 2a_{k-i}. If k-i=i, only one term j=i gives a_i^2; derivative 2a_i. Formula 2a_i. ```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_LBFGS_BETA = 1.0
_LBFGS_DEADLINE = 0.0


def _fft_convolve(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution a * a via real FFT."""
    n = len(a)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    if size <= 1:
        return np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    N = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
    return np.ascontiguousarray(c[:size])


def _score(a):
    """Internal exact score J(a)=2n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = len(a)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    c = _fft_convolve(a)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * M / (s * s))


def _normalize(a):
    """Non-negativize and normalize to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s


def _interpolate_sequence(a, n_target):
    """Resample a nonnegative sequence to a new length and normalize."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n_src = len(a)
    if n_src == n_target:
        return _normalize(a)
    if n_src == 1:
        return np.ones(n_target, dtype=np.float64) / n_target
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_src)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_target)
    out = np.interp(x1, x0, a)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return _normalize(out)


def _softmax(theta):
    """Stable softmax."""
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    t = theta - np.max(theta)
    t = np.maximum(t, -700.0)
    e = np.exp(t)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(theta.size, dtype=np.float64) / theta.size
    return e / s


def _corr_pa(a, p):
    """
    r_i = sum_j a_j p_{i+j}, for p length 2n-1.
    This is the sensitivity correlation needed for gradient computations.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    L = len(p)
    size = n + L - 1
    N = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * P, N)
    idx = L - 1 - np.arange(n)
    return np.ascontiguousarray(conv[idx])


def _ls_value(theta, beta):
    """
    Smooth objective logsumexp(beta*c) and its softmax-space gradient.
    c = self-convolution of softmax(theta).
    """
    try:
        theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
        t = theta - np.max(theta)
        t = np.maximum(t, -700.0)
        e = np.exp(t)
        s = float(np.sum(e))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            return 1e100, np.zeros_like(theta)

        a = e / s
        c = _fft_convolve(a)
        maxc = float(np.max(c))
        shifted = beta * (c - maxc)
        p = np.exp(shifted)
        Z = float(np.sum(p))
        if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 1e-300:
            return 1e100, np.zeros_like(theta)

        F = maxc + math.log(Z)

        r = _corr_pa(a, p)
        S = float(np.dot(a, r))
        grad = (2.0 * beta / Z) * a * (r - S)
        grad -= np.mean(grad)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            grad = np.zeros_like(theta)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm > 1e5:
            grad *= 1e5 / gnorm

        if not np.isfinite(F):
            F = 1e100
        return F, grad
    except Exception:
        try:
            theta = np.asarray(theta)
        except Exception:
            theta = np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(theta)


def _lbfgs_f(x):
    val, _ = _ls_value(x, _LBFGS_BETA)
    return val


def _lbfgs_g(x):
    _, g = _ls_value(x, _LBFGS_BETA)
    return g


def _lbfgs_callback(*args):
    return time.monotonic() > _LBFGS_DEADLINE


def _lbfgs_opt(a0, betas, deadline, maxfun=500):
    """
    L-BFGS-B optimization in log-space using smooth log-sum-exp objective.
    Keeps the best exact-sequence encountered.
    """
    global _LBFGS_BETA, _LBFGS_DEADLINE

    a = _normalize(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_s = _score(a)

    theta = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-15))
    theta -= np.mean(theta)

    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        b = float(beta)
        if not np.isfinite(b) or b <= 0.0:
            continue

        _LBFGS_BETA = b
        _LBFGS_DEADLINE = deadline

        options = {
            "maxiter": 300,
            "maxfun": int(maxfun),
            "ftol": 1e-14,
            "gtol": 1e-8,
            "maxls": 40,
            "disp": False,
        }

        try:
            res = minimize(
                _lbfgs_f,
                theta,
                jac=_lbfgs_g,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options=options,
                callback=_lbfgs_callback,
            )
        except Exception as ex:
            print(f"       LBFGS beta={b:.0f} failed: {ex}")
            break

        if hasattr(res, "x"):
            theta_new = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if theta_new.size == theta.size and np.all(np.isfinite(theta_new)):
                theta = theta_new
                a_new = _normalize(_softmax(theta_new))
                s_new = _score(a_new)
                if np.isfinite(s_new) and s_new < best_s - 1e-12:
                    best_s = s_new
                    best_a = a_new.copy()

    print(f"       LBFGS best={best_s:.8f}")
    return best_a, best_s


def _topk_direction(a, c, K):
    """
    Direction in log-space decreasing a top-peak weighted sum of convolution
    coefficients.  Returns a descent direction d for theta updates.
    """
    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    K = int(min(max(K, 1), L))

    if K >= L:
        order = np.arange(L)
    else:
        order = np.argpartition(-c, K - 1)[:K]

    vals = c[order]
    vmax = float(np.max(vals)) if vals.size else 0.0
    if vmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(vmax):
        w = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = (vals / vmax) ** 4.0
        sw = float(np.sum(w))
        if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 1e-300:
            w = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            w = w / sw

    p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    p[order] = w
    r = _corr_pa(a, p)
    S = float(np.dot(a, r))
    d = a * (S - r)
    d -= np.mean(d)
    return d


def _subgradient_topk(a, k_list, deadline, max_rounds=20):
    """
    Exact-sequence line search along top-peak log-space subgradient directions.
    """
    a = _normalize(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_s = _score(a)

    alphas = np.unique(
        np.concatenate(
            (
                np.logspace(-4.0, 0.8, 24),
                np.array([0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0], dtype=np.float64),
            )
        )
    )
    alphas = np.sort(alphas)

    for rnd in range(max_rounds):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        K = int(k_list[rnd % len(k_list)])
        c = _fft_convolve(a)
        d = _topk_direction(a, c, K)
        dn = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if not np.isfinite(dn) or dn < 1e-14:
            break
        d = d / dn

        theta = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
        improved = False

        for alpha in alphas:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            a_try = _softmax(theta + float(alpha) * d)
            s_try = _score(a_try)
            if np.isfinite(s_try) and s_try < best_s - 1e-12:
                best_s = s_try
                best_a = a_try.copy()
                improved = True

        if not improved:
            break
        a = best_a

    return best_a, best_s


def _coord_grid_points(n):
    """Number of 1D coordinate-grid points depends on length."""
    if n <= 700:
        p = 17
    elif n <= 1200:
        p = 13
    elif n <= 2000:
        p = 11
    else:
        p = 9
    return np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p)


def _coord_eval_scalar(gamma, h, idx, m):
    """Evaluate max coefficient after changing coordinate r to mass m."""
    one = 1.0 - m
    val = gamma * one * one + 2.0 * h * m * one
    if 0 <= idx < val.size:
        val[idx] += m * m
    return float(np.max(val))


def _coord_eval_vec(gamma, h, idx, ms):
    """Evaluate coordinate objective for multiple masses ms."""
    ms = np.asarray(ms, dtype=np.float64)
    one = 1.0 - ms
    val = gamma[None, :] * (one * one)[:, None] + 2.0 * h[None, :] * (ms * one)[:, None]
    if 0 <= idx < val.shape[1]:
        val[idx, :] += ms * ms
    return np.max(val, axis=1)


def _golden_coord(gamma, h, idx, lo, hi, iters):
    """Golden-section minimization for one coordinate scalar objective."""
    gr = (math.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0
    c = hi - gr * (hi - lo)
    d = lo + gr * (hi - lo)
    fc = _coord_eval_scalar(gamma, h, idx, c)
    fd = _coord_eval_scalar(gamma, h, idx, d)

    for _ in range(iters):
        if fc < fd:
            hi = d
            d = c
            fd = fc
            c = hi - gr * (hi - lo)
            fc = _coord_eval_scalar(gamma, h, idx, c)
        else:
            lo = c
            c = d
            fc = fd
            d = lo + gr * (hi - lo)
            fd = _coord_eval_scalar(gamma, h, idx, d)

    return 0.5 * (lo + hi)


def _coord_polish(a, deadline, max_sweeps=100, verbose=False):
    """
    Exact 1D coordinate descent for the normalized sequence.
    For fixed coordinate r, optimize its mass m in [0,1] exactly via
    multi-start golden-section on pieces of the 1D max-quadratic objective.
    """
    a = _normalize(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_s = _score(a)

    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    grid = _coord_grid_points(n)
    G = len(grid)
    refine_iters = 20 if n <= 1000 else 12
    tol = 1e-12

    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 3.0:
            break

        c = _fft_convolve(a)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        improved = 0

        for r in range(n):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
                break

            u = float(a[r])
            s1 = 1.0 - u
            if s1 <= 1e-12:
                continue

            # Remove coordinate r from current convolution to get gamma and h.
            num = c.copy()
            num[r:r + n] -= (2.0 * u) * a
            idx2 = 2 * r
            num[idx2] += u * u
            gamma = num / (s1 * s1)

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(gamma)):
                continue
            gamma = np.where(gamma < 0.0, 0.0, gamma)

            h = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            h[r:r + n] = a / s1
            h[idx2] = 0.0

            vals = _coord_eval_vec(gamma, h, idx2, grid)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(vals)):
                continue

            Mg = float(np.min(vals))
            iMg = int(np.argmin(vals))
            Mu = _coord_eval_scalar(gamma, h, idx2, u)
            M_best = min(Mg, Mu, M)

            if M_best < M - tol:
                m_best = float(u) if Mu <= Mg else float(grid[iMg])
                candidates = []

                # Local minima on the coarse grid.
                for i in range(G):
                    if vals[i] < M - tol:
                        left = vals[i - 1] if i > 0 else np.inf
                        right = vals[i + 1] if i < G - 1 else np.inf
                        if vals[i] <= left + 1e-15 and vals[i] <= right + 1e-15:
                            if i == 0:
                                lo = 0.0
                                hi = grid[1] if G > 1 else 0.25
                            elif i == G - 1:
                                lo = grid[G - 2] if G > 1 else 0.75
                                hi = 1.0
                            else:
                                lo = grid[i - 1]
                                hi = grid[i + 1]
                            if hi > lo + 1e-9:
                                candidates.append((float(vals[i]), float(lo), float(hi)))

                # Include current point.
                if Mu < M - tol:
                    step = 1.0 / (G - 1) if G > 1 else 0.25
                    lo = max(0.0, u - step)
                    hi = min(1.0, u + step)
                    if hi > lo + 1e-9:
                        candidates.append((Mu, float(lo), float(hi)))

                # Include the best grid point even if not detected as local.
                if Mg < M - tol:
                    step = 1.0 / (G - 1) if G > 1 else 0.25
                    lo = max(0.0, float(grid[iMg]) - step)
                    hi = min(1.0, float(grid[iMg]) + step)
                    if hi > lo + 1e-9:
                        candidates.append((Mg, float(lo), float(hi)))

                if candidates:
                    candidates.sort()
                    for _, lo, hi in candidates[:3]:
                        if hi > lo + 1e-9:
                            m_ref = _golden_coord(gamma, h, idx2, lo, hi, refine_iters)
                            M_ref = _coord_eval_scalar(gamma, h, idx2, m_ref)
                            if M_ref < M_best - 1e-15:
                                M_best = M_ref
                                m_best = m_ref

                if M_best < M - 1e-12:
                    one = 1.0 - m_best
                    a_new = one * (a / s1)
                    a_new[r] = m_best
                    a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
                    sn = float(np.sum(a_new))

                    if sn > 1e-300:
                        a_new = a_new / sn
                        c_new = _fft_convolve(a_new)
                        M_new = float(np.max(c_new))

                        if np.isfinite(M_new) and M_new < M - 1e-13:
                            a = a_new
                            c = c_new
                            M = M_new
                            improved += 1

                            s_est = 2.0 * n * M
                            if s_est < best_s - 1e-12:
                                best_s = s_est
                                best_a = a.copy()

                            if verbose and improved % 50 == 0:
                                print(f"        coord sweep {sweep}: improved={improved}, score={best_s:.8f}")

        if improved == 0:
            break

    s_final = _score(best_a)
    return best_a, s_final


def _optimize_length(a_start, n, deadline, verbose=False):
    """
    Multi-stage optimizer for a fixed length:
      1. L-BFGS-B log-sum-exp
      2. exact top-peak subgradient line search
      3. coordinate descent polish
    Repeat a few times while improving.
    """
    a = _interpolate_sequence(a_start, n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_s = _score(a)

    max_cycles = 3 if n <= 700 else 2
    klist = [1, 1, 2, 2, 4, 4, 8, 16, 32]

    if verbose:
        print(f"     Optimize length n={n}, start score={best_s:.8f}")

    for cyc in range(max_cycles):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
            break

        improved_cycle = False

        M = best_s / (2.0 * n) if n > 0 else 1.0
        if M <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(M):
            M = 1e-6

        betas = [
            max(1.0, 5.0 / M),
            max(1.0, 15.0 / M),
            max(1.0, 35.0 / M),
        ]

        if n <= 600:
            maxfun = 650
        elif n <= 1000:
            maxfun = 350
        else:
            maxfun = 220

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        alloc_lb = min(rem * 0.40, 280.0)
        if alloc_lb > 25.0:
            a1, s1 = _lbfgs_opt(a, betas, time.monotonic() + alloc_lb, maxfun=maxfun)
            if np.isfinite(s1):
                a = a1
                if s1 < best_s - 1e-12:
                    best_s = s1
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    improved_cycle = True

        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
            break

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        alloc_sg = min(rem * 0.15, 80.0)
        if alloc_sg > 20.0:
            ms_rounds = 30 if n <= 800 else 12
            a2, s2 = _subgradient_topk(a, klist, time.monotonic() + alloc_sg, max_rounds=ms_rounds)
            if np.isfinite(s2):
                a = a2
                if s2 < best_s - 1e-12:
                    best_s = s2
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    improved_cycle = True

        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
            break

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        alloc_cd = max(0.0, rem - 10.0)
        cap_cd = 360.0 if n <= 700 else 240.0
        alloc_cd = min(alloc_cd, cap_cd)
        if alloc_cd > 25.0:
            msweeps = 400 if n <= 700 else 150
            a3, s3 = _coord_polish(a, time.monotonic() + alloc_cd, max_sweeps=msweeps, verbose=verbose)
            if np.isfinite(s3):
                a = a3
                if s3 < best_s - 1e-12:
                    best_s = s3
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    improved_cycle = True

        if verbose:
            print(f"     cycle {cyc + 1} n={n} score={best_s:.8f}")

        if not improved_cycle:
            break

    final_s = _score(best_a)
    return best_a, final_s


def propose_candidate():
    """
    Hybrid sequence optimiser:
    - L-BFGS-B in log-space on log-sum-exp of convolution peaks
    - exact top-peak subgradient line searches
    - exact one-coordinate descent polishing
    - multi-length exploration and final polish.
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = start + 2350.0

    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # Load current best if present.
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if parent.ndim != 1 or parent.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(parent)):
            parent = None
        else:
            parent = _normalize(parent)
            ps = _score(parent)
            if np.isfinite(ps):
                best_a = parent.copy()
                best_score = ps
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if best_a is None:
        n0 = 500
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n0)
        a0 = (1.0 - t) * (1.0 + 0.25 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * 3.0 * t))
        best_a = _normalize(a0)
        best_score = _score(best_a)

    print(f"Initial best: n={len(best_a)}, score={best_score:.8f}")

    # Main working length: use parent when moderate, otherwise 500.
    n_parent = len(best_a)
    if 180 <= n_parent <= 850:
        n_main = n_parent
    else:
        n_main = 500

    a_main = _interpolate_sequence(best_a, n_main)
    s_main = _score(a_main)
    if np.isfinite(s_main) and s_main < best_score - 1e-12:
        best_score = s_main
        best_a = a_main.copy()

    main_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 380.0, start + 1300.0)
    if time.monotonic() < main_deadline - 60.0:
        a_opt, s_opt = _optimize_length(a_main, n_main, main_deadline, verbose=True)
        if np.isfinite(s_opt):
            if s_opt < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = s_opt
                best_a = a_opt.copy()
            else:
                # even if no improvement in reported score, keep available
                pass

    # Explore other lengths.  Larger lengths can be closer to the asymptotic ceiling.
    other_lengths = []
    for nn in [600, 800, 1000, 1200, 400, 700, 900, 300]:
        if nn != n_main:
            other_lengths.append(nn)

    for nn in other_lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 260.0:
            break
        alloc = min(260.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 220.0)
        if alloc < 80.0:
            break
        a_start = _interpolate_sequence(best_a, nn)
        a_opt, s_opt = _optimize_length(a_start, nn, time.monotonic() + alloc, verbose=False)
        if np.isfinite(s_opt) and s_opt < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = s_opt
            best_a = a_opt.copy()

    # Final coordinate polish on the globally best sequence.
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 20.0:
        n_best = len(best_a)
        a_final = _interpolate_sequence(best_a, n_best)
        a_opt, s_opt = _coord_polish(a_final, hard_deadline - 5.0, max_sweeps=200, verbose=False)
        if np.isfinite(s_opt) and s_opt < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = s_opt
            best_a = a_opt.copy()

    arr = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    result = [float(x) for x in arr]

    try:
        ev = float(evaluate_sequence(result))
        print(f"Final returned score via evaluator: {ev:.8f}, n={len(result)}")
    except Exception:
        ev = float("inf")
        print("Evaluator verification unavailable/failed")

    if not np.isfinite(ev):
        result = [1.0] * max(2, len(result))

    return result
```
